export const meta = {
  name: 'step15b-table-of-record-vc1',
  description: 'Values-check pass 1 of the sentence table of record: rows, completeness and mechanics, each with a strict refuter',
  phases: [{ title: 'Check' }, { title: 'Refute' }],
}
const S2 = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15b'
const R = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. You values-check the sentence table of record for PROPOSAL-m's Q-F0 v1 posting, before its commit (repo ${R}, HEAD 2f46fa8).
The table: ${S2}/tor/m_posting_sentences.json (kind of-record; 56 rows; sha256 4ddcc640…86b3; to be committed as
data/m/gates/m_posting_sentences.json). It names the committed preprint of record ${R}/PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (patch v8,
45b67246…7a41). Its classification is Code's (PIN-20), under Code's spec ${S2}/table/spec.md (the rules are Code's readings, put to
the seat in results/raw/2026-10-15_m-code-to-design_step15b.md, Q1/Q2: you check the table AGAINST THE RULES AS DECLARED, not the
rules themselves; where you think a rule itself is wrong, say so in a note, but judge rows by the rules). Each row's rationale of
record: ${S2}/tor/rationales_of_record.json (48 the adjudicator's; 8 Code's, for the rows Code's assembly changed: 61.3, 30.2, 33.2,
186.2, 236.1, 248.2, 254.2, 276.5). The units (the declared segmenter's): ${S2}/table/sentences_v8.txt and .json; the segmenter
${S2}/table/segment.py. The draft's classification history (three classifiers, adjudicator, two critics, Code's assembly) is in
${R}/results/raw/2026-10-15_m-analyzer-round2-step15_review.txt (section THE TABLE DRAFT AND PIN-17) and the table raw
${R}/results/raw/2026-10-15_m-table-draft-step15.txt. The analyzer of record is ${R}/scripts/m_analyze.py at HEAD (655e0f7f…c16f):
table_problems, enumeration, CELLS, KINDS_VOCAB. Builder and tally: ${S2}/table/build_table.py; conventions: ${S2}/table/check_conventions.py.
To run python on the analyzer: cd to your work dir and use PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONPATH=${S2.replace('step15b', 'step12')}/mt3/net
${R}/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python -s -B (the net blocks model imports and sockets; import the analyzer from ${R}/scripts/m_analyze.py
by importlib with sys.modules registered, read-only).
HARD RULES. Read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt; never run git in
jlens-qwen36/; modify nothing in ${R} (write only under ${S2}/tvc1/); do not read scripts/audit_note_*, results/raw/*note-*,
README-NOTE.md, RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg. Outcome-blind: no campaign result exists or is needed.`
const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, row: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
    finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'row', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'rows', task: `For EVERY one of the 56 rows: (i) id, loc (line, sentence, section) and text against the copy and the segmenter's unit (exact); (ii) null_cells and thesis against the spec's rules (a) and (b), reading the unit in its paragraph; (iii) dose_note and its span (verbatim, the spec's extent) and kinds against the spec's kinds map; (iv) the rationale of record: does it argue exactly the row's classification (cells and thesis), and is every quotation in it verbatim from the copy? List every row you would change, with the rule that requires it, and every rationale that does not match its row.` },
  { key: 'completeness', task: `Go through EVERY unit of sentences_v8.txt outside the spec's excluded regions that is NOT a row, and decide under the spec's inclusion rule whether it must be one. Be adversarial on the costly error: an omitted unit that asserts a null on any cell (especially e2b, the weight channel or the OLMo base row) or states a thesis. Also check the excluded regions are exactly the spec's (line 1; the header note 458-468; the References 304-450) and that nothing posted falls inside them.` },
  { key: 'mechanics', task: `(i) Run the committed analyzer's table_problems on the table, reading the committed preprint (expect []); (ii) run check_conventions.py; (iii) recompute each row's fires (a), (b), (c) under both flags over the 1,080 tuples with the committed analyzer's enumeration and compare with the table raw's report; (iv) confirm the file is resume.json_text(table, indent=1) byte for byte, that kinds_vocabulary and cells equal the analyzer's, and that rebuilding it from the rows file with build_table.py on HEAD's analyzer reproduces it byte for byte; (v) probe the table against the posting path's own reader if the analyzer exposes one that runs without a lock (do not write outside your work dir).` },
]
const res = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK (${l.key}): ${l.task} Work dir ${S2}/tvc1/${l.key}.`, { label: 'tvc1:' + l.key, phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (${l.key}), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it against the sources and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with your evidence and the exact fix. Default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-verify 3 of its 'verified' items. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in the table that it did not find. Work dir ${S2}/tvc1/ref_${l.key}.`, { label: 'tvc1-ref:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VER }).then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null)
return res

# Code's dispositions of the table of record's values-check, pass 1 (three lenses, each with a strict refuter)

The table is revised as below. The revision is 55 rows, sha256 7206d78b…d0c5; the draft v2 it revises was 56 rows,
4ddcc640…86b3. table_problems on the committed analyzer returns [], and every convention holds. Fires change only for
R240.03, (a) 1,079 → 1,080, and for R166.04, which is dropped and was inert. 17 rows now fire (a) in every tuple.

## Applied (rows)

- **T1** (PARTLY; the refuter's form): R240.03 gains olmo-base under the ambiguity rule. The silent-lever screen ran
  at base (184.2), and the paragraph's 240.2 and 240.6 reach the base row. e2b stays out by the spec's state-live-read
  clause. The rationale is rewritten.
- **T3** (CONFIRMED): R033.02, R248.02 and R276.05 gain olmo-base. "The nulls" and "the uninstructed nulls" are read
  as R254.02 reads "Every null": the ontogeny nulls are included under the ambiguity rule, and the base screen is
  uninstructed too (184.6). Rationales are amended. No fires change, since each row already fired in 1,080 through e2b.
- **T4** (CONFIRMED): R166.04 is dropped. "State presence and self-recognizability dissociate in both directions" is
  none of rule (a)'s kinds, and the unit carries no thesis, dose note or kind. R262.05 loses S1: "a transcript the
  model-as-judge cannot read" is a recognition result. Both are inert. R254.02 keeps S1: "Every null" includes S1's
  labeled null (180.3's "*Answered, as recognition*", kind 1). Its rationale now rests on that, not on 262.5.

## Applied (rationales of record; no row value changes)

- **T5** (PARTLY): R186.04's firing-equivalence sentence is deleted; f1 rests on the vanilla-read mapping it already
  cites. R240.03's is gone with its rewrite.
- **T6, T7** (CONFIRMED): quotations made byte-exact. R184.08's label is quoted in full; R234.01 regains its
  "functional" quotes. The bold ** around bracket tags is restored in R172.07, R182.12, R186.02, R220.01, R232.02,
  R234.06, R240.06 and R244.02. The backticks are restored in R003.01's spec quote and R270.01's quote of the seat.
  R030.02's gloss is unquoted.
- **T12, M1** (CONFIRMED): the rationales of record are not in the table, whose row keys are fixed. They are committed
  verbatim in the table's values-check raw, with each row's boundary mark and whose rationale it is. They supersede the
  table raw's ROWS rationales (ed8e886) for the rows they change.

(ii) Then sweep EVERY row whose cells rest on scope words or presuppositions ("the nulls", "every null", "our data", "these lineages", "the model", "this paper", lineage-wide or data-wide phrases, references back) and check the ambiguity rule and the spec's e2b clause ("e2b where the unit includes the state-live read") are applied to all of them alike after the changes: any row that by the same reasoning should now gain or lose e2b or olmo-base? (iii) Every rationale of record: does it argue exactly its row's cells and thesis, and is every quotation in it byte-exact from its source?` },
  { key: 'mechanics-completeness', task: `(i) Re-run the committed analyzer's table_problems (expect []), check_conventions.py, and the per-row fires over 1,080 tuples x both flags; confirm the report_of_record.txt and that the 17 rows firing (a) in every tuple are exactly those carrying e2b, the weight channel or the base row; confirm resume.json_text(indent=1) byte form and a rebuild reproduces it. (ii) Completeness, fresh: read every non-row unit outside the excluded regions (now including 166.4) and decide under the inclusion rule as declared whether it must be a row; adversarial on omitted nulls on e2b, the weight channel or the base row and on theses.` },
]
const res = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK (${l.key}): ${l.task} Work dir ${S2}/tvc2/${l.key}.`, { label: 'tvc2:' + l.key, phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (${l.key}), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with your evidence and the exact fix; default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-verify 3 'verified' items. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in the table it did not find. Work dir ${S2}/tvc2/ref_${l.key}.`, { label: 'tvc2-ref:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VER }).then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null)
return res

# Code's dispositions of the table of record's values-check, pass 2 (two lenses, each with a strict refuter)

Pass 2 checked pass 1's revision (55 rows, sha256 7206d78b…d0c5; rows of record a7bfddb0…57e8bd). Its rule-level
findings are four rows where applying the table's own practice consistently would add cells (three of them, all but
R236.02, with an alternative on record that keeps their values with a stated ground), and four lines that practice
leaves open. On the PI's word (chat, 2026-10-15: the PI selected Code's option "Hold table, rest proceeds
(Recommended)" over "Hold everything" and "Close the strict rule now"), the table of record is held for the seat's
Q2, and those findings are routed in Code's addendum to the step-15b ping, not applied. The fixes that move no value
are applied. The rows of record become rows_of_record.v2.json (15ca089b…f420). The table rebuilt from them is
byte-identical (7206d78b…d0c5), and its report is byte-identical to the report of record.

---- q2/readings2.py, verbatim
"""The table of record's candidate (pass-1 revision) under the readings Q2 puts to the seat, on the committed analyzer
(read-only import). For each reading: rows, table_problems, rows firing clause (a) in 1,080 / 1,079 / fewer, the
tuples where they differ, and the enumeration at the all-PASS tuple under both flags.
usage: python3 -s -B readings2.py <repo> <rows_of_record.json> <sentences_v8.json> <outdir> [versions,comma-separated]"""
import copy, hashlib, importlib.util, itertools, json, sys
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
REPO, ROWS, SENT, OUT = map(Path, sys.argv[1:5])
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("m_an_q2", REPO / "scripts/m_analyze.py")
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules["m_an_q2"] = m; spec.loader.exec_module(m)
from valence_core.m import resume  # noqa: E402
print("analyzer sha256", hashlib.sha256((REPO / "scripts/m_analyze.py").read_bytes()).hexdigest())
PRE = "PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md"; data = (REPO / PRE).read_bytes()
sent = {f'{s["line"]}.{s["sentence"]}': s for s in json.load(open(SENT, encoding="utf-8"))}
rows0 = json.load(open(ROWS, encoding="utf-8"))
GEN = ["e2a", "f1", "olmo-sft", "olmo-dpo", "olmo-instruct"]
def build(rows_in):
    rows = []
    for r in sorted(rows_in, key=lambda x: tuple(int(v) for v in x["unit"].split("."))):
        s = sent[r["unit"]]; text = s["text"]
        rows.append({"id": f'R{s["line"]:03d}.{s["sentence"]:02d}',
                     "loc": {"line": s["line"], "section": s["section"], "sentence": s["sentence"]},
                     "text": text, "text_sha256": hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
                     "null_cells": [c for c in m.CELLS if c in r["null_cells"]],
                     "thesis": r["thesis"], "dose_note": bool(r["dose_note"]),
                     "dose_note_span": r["dose_note_span"] if r["dose_note"] else None,
                     "kinds": [k for k in m.KINDS_VOCAB if k in r["kinds"]]})
    return {"schema": m.TABLE_SCHEMA, "kind": "of-record", "posting": "v1",
            "preprint": {"path": PRE, "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "bytes": len(data),
                         "lines": data.decode("utf-8").count("\n")},
            "cells": list(m.CELLS), "kinds_vocabulary": list(m.KINDS_VOCAB), "rows": rows}
NAMES = sys.argv[5].split(",") if len(sys.argv) > 5 else ["B", "as-is", "pass2", "pass2+186.4", "pass2+5.1", "pass2+5.1+186.4", "A3"]
def variant(name):
    rs = copy.deepcopy(rows0)
    by = {r["unit"]: r for r in rs}
    if name == "as-is":
        pass
    elif name == "pass2":          # pass 2's findings applied: K1 option (1) taken, K2 with R234.04, R123.01
        by["248.6"]["null_cells"] = sorted(set(by["248.6"]["null_cells"]) | {"e2b", "olmo-base"})
        by["236.2"]["null_cells"] = sorted(set(by["236.2"]["null_cells"]) | {"olmo-base"})
        by["234.4"]["null_cells"] = sorted(set(by["234.4"]["null_cells"]) | {"olmo-base"})
        by["123.1"]["null_cells"] = sorted(set(by["123.1"]["null_cells"]) | {"e2b", "olmo-base"})
    elif name == "closure":        # every row whose cells are exactly the general list takes the base row
        for r in rs:
            if sorted(r["null_cells"]) == sorted(GEN):
                r["null_cells"] = sorted(set(r["null_cells"]) | {"olmo-base"})
    elif name == "closure-minus-13.11":   # the bound without R013.11, whose text states its stages
        for r in rs:
            if sorted(r["null_cells"]) == sorted(GEN) and r["unit"] != "13.11":
                r["null_cells"] = sorted(set(r["null_cells"]) | {"olmo-base"})

        "OLMo-sft": m.not_run_reason("OLMo-sft", "clock:no-box", m.CLOCK_DATE),
        "OLMo-dpo": m.not_run_reason("OLMo-dpo", "dose-not-completable"),
        "OLMo-instruct": m.not_run_reason("OLMo-instruct", "clock:gate-stopped:G-TIME-OLMo-instruct", m.CLOCK_DATE)}
res = {}
for name in NAMES:
    tab = build(variant(name))
    probs = m.table_problems(tab, read_repo_file=lambda p: (REPO / p).read_bytes() if (REPO / p).is_file() else None)
    txt = resume.json_text(tab, indent=1)
    (OUT / f"table_{name}.json").write_text(txt, encoding="utf-8")
    fa = Counter(); per_tuple = {}
    n = 0
    for t in itertools.product(m.Q5, m.O6, m.O6, m.O6):
        rsn = {u: whys[u] for u, x in zip(m.UNITS, t) if x == "NOT-RUN"}
        n += 1
        it = m.enumeration(t, True, tab, rsn)
        a = [x["row_id"] for x in it if "a" in x["clauses"]]
        for rid in a: fa[rid] += 1
        per_tuple[t] = (len(it), len(a))
    ids = [r["id"] for r in tab["rows"]]
    a1080 = [i for i in ids if fa[i] == 1080]; a1079 = [i for i in ids if fa[i] == 1079]
    allpass = {}
    for f in (True, False):
        it = m.enumeration(("PASS",) * 4, f, tab, {})
        allpass[f] = {"n": len(it), "a": [x["row_id"] for x in it if "a" in x["clauses"]],
                      "b": [x["row_id"] for x in it if "b" in x["clauses"]],
                      "c": [x["row_id"] for x in it if "c" in x["clauses"]]}
    # tuples where the (a) count differs from as-is are computed below
    res[name] = {"rows": len(ids), "table_problems": probs, "sha256": hashlib.sha256(txt.encode()).hexdigest(),
                 "tuples": n, "a_1080": a1080, "a_1079": a1079,
                 "a_other": {i: fa[i] for i in ids if 0 < fa[i] < 1079},
                 "allpass": {str(k): v for k, v in allpass.items()}, "_pt": per_tuple}
base_pt = res["as-is"]["_pt"]
for name in res:
    pt = res[name].pop("_pt")
    diff = [t for t in pt if pt[t][1] != base_pt[t][1]]
    res[name]["tuples_where_a_count_differs_from_as_is"] = [list(t) for t in diff]
json.dump(res, open(OUT / ("readings.json" if len(sys.argv) <= 5 else "readings_extra.json"), "w"), indent=1)
for name, r in res.items():
    ap = r["allpass"]
    u = lambda f: sorted(set(ap[f]["a"]) | set(ap[f]["b"]) | set(ap[f]["c"]))
    print(f'== {name}: rows {r["rows"]} problems {r["table_problems"]} sha {r["sha256"][:16]}')
    print(f'   (a) in 1080: {len(r["a_1080"])}  in 1079: {len(r["a_1079"])}  other: {r["a_other"]}')
    print(f'   tuples whose (a) count differs from as-is: {r["tuples_where_a_count_differs_from_as_is"]}')
    for f in ("True", "False"):
        print(f'   all-PASS flag {f}: items {ap[f]["n"]}; (a) {len(ap[f]["a"])} (b) {len(ap[f]["b"])} (c) {ap[f]["c"]}; a&b {sorted(set(ap[f]["a"]) & set(ap[f]["b"]))}')
    print(f'   (a) at all-PASS: {ap["True"]["a"]}')

"""Pass 2's non-rule fixes (K4, K5, K6, MC4) on the rows of record: rationale texts and rationale_by only; no row
value changes. In: rows_of_record.json (a7bfddb0...57e8bd). Out: rows_of_record.v2.json."""
import json, sys
rows = json.load(open(sys.argv[1], encoding="utf-8")); by = {r["unit"]: r for r in rows}
def sub(u, old, new):
    t = by[u]["rationale"]; assert t.count(old) == 1, (u, old); by[u]["rationale"] = t.replace(old, new)
# K4: the spec's mapping as written
sub("33.2", "The spec maps 'the self-administration nulls' to e2a and e2b",
            "The spec maps 'the self-administration cells/nulls' to e2a and e2b")
# K6: why S1 stays out
sub("33.2", "beside e2a, f1 and the three post-training rows (the values-check's T3).",
            "beside e2a, f1 and the three post-training rows (the values-check's T3). S1 stays out: 'a missing consumer or a general inability' does not fit a result closed at recognition (180.3).")
sub("248.2", "beside e2a, f1 and the three post-training rows (the values-check's T3).",
             "beside e2a, f1 and the three post-training rows (the values-check's T3); S1 stays out, as at 33.2, since 'in-context learning rather than motive' does not fit a result closed at recognition.")
# K5: byte-exact quotations
sub("240.3", "e2b stays out by the spec's clause 'e2b where the unit includes the state-live read'.",
             "e2b stays out by the spec's clause on e2b (spec.md:60-61, which breaks the line after 'includes': 'e2b where the unit includes the state-live read').")
sub("254.2", "*Answered, as recognition* (180.3)", "*Answered, as recognition.* (180.3)")
# MC4: provenance markers
for u, tag in (("30.2", "T7"), ("186.2", "T7"), ("254.2", "T4; pass 2's K5"), ("33.2", "T3; pass 2's K4 and K6"),
               ("248.2", "T3; pass 2's K6"), ("276.5", "T3")):
    assert by[u]["rationale_by"] == "Code (the assembly)", (u, by[u]["rationale_by"])
    by[u]["rationale_by"] = f"Code (the assembly), as amended by Code (the values-check, {tag})"
assert by["240.3"]["rationale_by"] == "Code (the values-check)"
by["240.3"]["rationale_by"] = "Code (the values-check, T1; pass 2's K5)"
